Every bet and raise is exactly 2 before the flop and on the flop, and 4 on the turn and the river; a betting round has at most 4 bets (the opening bet, or the big blind before the flop, included). On your turn you fold, check, call, bet or raise with act. The best five-card hand from your two cards and the board wins the pot at the showdown; if everyone else folds, the last player wins it without showing. Every hand is scored alone: you can always cover the betting (there is no all-in), and your points from a hand are what you win minus what you put in. Cards are written rank then suit: ranks 2-9, T (ten), J, Q, K, A; suits s (spades), h (hearts), d (diamonds), c (clubs).
